     PLAN — the written Results, re-mapped onto two figures, panel by panel

     Context

     The author has written the Results into the Google Doc (MK_Myc_Paper, tab
     Gyorgy
     Writing, read this session). The written text deviates from
     docs/2026-08-02_results_narrative_final_order_D.md, which had commissioned
     Figures 2 and 3
     as 2A–2D / 3A–3D. The written version:

     - keeps one main figure for the oncogene section and folds the attenuation
     section into it
     — Figure 1 grows from B/C/D to B–H;
     - puts everything from the MYC-ER model onwards into Figure 2 (2A–2I);
     - adds S1D–S1F and S2A–S2D;
     - frees the Fig. 1E slot (the superseded partial-correlation paragraph is
     gone) and reuses
     it for mitochondrial content;
     - retires Figure 3 entirely — "the whole narrative has to fit into two
     figures with 2
     supplementaries".

     So the job is (1) re-map every slot from the written sentences, (2) rewrite
     PANELS.md to
     that map, and (3) build the panels one at a time: I write the script, the
     author runs it in
     Positron, looks, modifies; when it is signed off we move to the next.

     Two decisions taken this session by the author: Fig. 1E draws both rulers 
     in two parts; the
     Myc × OXPHOS-priority → PUMA:Bcl-xL coupling does get a panel, as Fig. 2I.

     ---
     CURRENT PANEL — Fig. 2E, figures/panels/fig2_wt_teb_proliferation.R

     Context

     The sentence, as written in the Doc:

     ▎ "The 6>12W_wt comparison showed that while the TEB signature was lost as 
     ▎ expected in the
     ▎ puberty-adult transition, the overall proliferation signalling remained 
     ▎ relatively stable
     ▎ (Fig. 2E)."

     This is the first panel of section 2 and it does one job: establish that
     what the normal gland
     loses between six and twelve weeks is a morphogenetic programme, not a 
     proliferative one. The
     whole of Figure 2 rests on the substrate having changed developmentally —
     the MYC-ER mice never
     saw Myc — so this panel is where that change is named. It is also the
     negative limb that keeps
     2F honest: if proliferation had collapsed here, the respiratory withdrawal
     of 2F would be its
     shadow rather than a finding.

     Ruler — author's call, 2026-08-04: the per-set fGSEA NES on timepoint_neg

     The same instrument as Figs. 1D and 1H, so "enrichment" means one thing
     across the figure set.
     One ranked list, so the normaliser is shared — the within-contrast
     comparison this panel makes
     is safe in exactly the way Fig. 1H's cross-contrast one was not, and the
     script says so.

     Numbers, read off results/fgsea_percategory.rds this session (no re-run):

     ┌────────────────────────────────────┬────────┬────────────┬─────────────┐
     │                row                 │ n sets │ median NES │ padj < 0.05 │
     ├────────────────────────────────────┼────────┼────────────┼─────────────┤
     │ TEB programmes                     │ 41     │ −1.56      │ 30          │
     ├────────────────────────────────────┼────────┼────────────┼─────────────┤
     │ TEB x MitoCarta lanes (*_TEB_MITO) │ 12     │ +0.49      │ 0           │
     ├────────────────────────────────────┼────────┼────────────┼─────────────┤
     │ proliferation (PROLIF_*)           │ 14     │ −0.89      │ 0           │
     └────────────────────────────────────┴────────┴────────────┴─────────────┘

     The anchor is MG_TEB_VS_DUCTAL_HS_GRAY_UP at NES −2.755, p 3.4e−18 —
     labelled on the panel,
     because it is the one set all three rulers share.

     What goes in the legend block, not on the page

     - Three rulers agree on the TEB arm and two of them are independent of 
     composition.
     gene-level set-average raw log2FC −0.4220, the 0th percentile of 2000
     expression-matched
     random sets (substrate_specificity_tradeoff.rds$wt_null); purity-adjusted
     per-sample composite
     (s ~ tp*myc + epi + imm) −0.4200, p 0.0053
     (priming_arm_teb.rds$teb_signatures). They
     agree to 0.002, so contamination does not explain the TEB loss.
     - "Relatively stable" is right on magnitude and needs one qualifier.
     PROLIF_* pooled is
     −0.047 log2, an 8.9-fold smaller move than TEB — but it sits at the 1.3rd 
     percentile of
     its own matched null (p = 0.013). It is small, not immobile, which is
     script 43's own wording
     and the one thing the sentence should absorb.
     - The construction row is drawn, not deleted — Fig. 1D's rule. Deleting it
     would flatter the
     TEB result; folding it into the TEB row would dilute it. They are MitoCarta
     subsets by build and
     17–59 genes each.
     - The generic proliferation regulons agree with the curated sets
     (TFT_E2F1_DOROTHEA_ABC
     −1.18 n.s., TFT_E2F1_CHIPATLAS +0.86 n.s.) — but the Gray _LE context lanes
     of the same
     transcription factors fall hard (FOXM1_HS_LE −2.10, MYBL2_HS_LE −2.04,
     E2F1_HS_LE −1.72, all
     padj < 1e−3). Those are lineage-identity lanes, not cell-cycle sets. What 
     moves in the normal
     gland is lineage and morphogenesis; the cell cycle itself does not — which
     is the same reading
     Fig. 1H's off-diagonal clouds gave, arriving where the narrative wants it.
     - Panel-scope BH over the 67 drawn sets changes nothing: the same 30 TEB
     sets survive and the best
     proliferation set reaches 0.061. Drawn significance stays
     padj_within_category, the
     analysis of record, and the two BH scopes are named.

     Bounds

     - batch = timepoint, so every value here is described, not claimed. The
     mitigation is
     that a batch effect would have to be TEB-specific to produce this: both
     families ride the
     same two batches, and the comparison — not either arm alone — is what the
     sentence makes.
     - fGSEA gene permutation is anti-conservative for correlated sets, which is
     most of this library.
     - BH is applied within category by script 20's design and the TEB family
     spans two categories
     (03_mammary_development, 06_tf_targets).

     Form

     Single plot, 89 x 46 mm, three rows, x = NES on 6>12W_wt. Solid zero line,
     dashed guides at
     NES = ±1 (the scale of an unmoved set — NES is the enrichment score over
     the mean of the
     same-signed permutation null). Shape is significance — filled at padj <
     0.05, open otherwise,
     which is Fig. 1F's declared encoding and the project rule that significance
     is never a fill or a
     point colour. Row medians as a short vertical rule. One ink; n in each row
     label; the anchor set
     labelled with ggrepel. Jitter with a fixed seed (fig04 panel D's idiom) —
     no new dependency.
     Nothing new is added to _panel_common.R: programme_group() is not the
     grouping here (it would
     scatter the TEB lanes across five rows), so the three families are defined
     in the script from
     substrate_specificity_tradeoff.rds$defs$prolif_sets and a TEB name match,
     and the roster
     identity is asserted.

     Assertions

     stopifnot on: the 14 drawn proliferation sets being setequal to
     $defs$prolif_sets
     (verified TRUE); 41 / 12 / 14 row counts; the anchor set present with its
     NES; $wt_null and
     $comparator agreeing on c_wt_time for both arms; and fig04's stale-object
     guard —
     $comparator OXPHOS-subunit wild type within 0.02 of
     $defs$oxphos_wt_reference (−0.255).
     require_fresher_than() on fgsea_percategory.rds (12:37, newer than
     gsva_scores.rds at 12:36
     — it passes, unlike the 1F case).

     ---
     What the six built panels changed in the text (all recorded in PANELS.md)

     Each build checked its sentence against the data first. Four sentences need
     a word changed and one
     needs rewriting — none of it changes a result.

     panel: 1E
     what it found: "94% of 143" mixes the 144-row and 143-row fractions;
     "21–27%"
     is narrower than the arms drawn (+20 to +33%); the padj < 0.001 is a pooled

     genotype effect and only biogenesis clears 0.05 at six weeks alone
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     panel: 1F
     what it found: "significantly promoted … OXPHOS subunits (all complexes)" 
     fails
     — OXPHOS subunits padj 0.27 and not one complex clears 0.05; the only
     significant OXPHOS row is CIII assembly factors at −0.141, demoted. Glycine

     cleavage (0.118) and mitophagy (0.084) also miss
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     panel: 1G
     what it found: the R2=*** placeholders are filled — and the p attaches to 
     the 
     R², not the slope (64 of 500 shuffles reach the slope, none reach the R²)
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     panel: 1H
     what it found: "enhanced" does not survive. The rise is global (81% of all
     sets, 93% of the enriched), the two named families rise by the median
     amount,
      and a global NES rise is what a flatter 12W ranking mechanically produces.

     What is true: they hold their position while the amplitude halves
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     panel: S1E
     what it found: "the expression gap remained constant between 6W_myc and 
     12W_myc" — a gap is between genotypes, not ages; both readings are true and

     the panel draws both, but say which
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     panel: S1F
     what it found: clean negative; nothing to change

     figures/panels/panels_to_pdf.R →
     outputs/figures/panels/Figure1_and_S1_panels.pdf, twelve
     pages, one panel each at true size, slot as the page title, for circulating
     beside the Doc text.

     PANELS.md — DONE, and kept current per panel

     Rewritten 2026-08-04 and extended with a signed-off entry per panel. The
     original brief was:
     replace the "Planned — Figures 2 and 3 (Order D)" section with the map
     above;
     move the built rows into a Figure-1 / Supplementary-1 table that carries
     the written
     sentences, not the pre-writing ones; move figS1_mb_fork_specificity.R to a
     "built, no slot
     yet" row; and carry forward the three unresolved text items (1D tier order
     E2F +2.08 above
     biosynthetic metabolism +1.84; "aligned almost entirely" →
     mitochondria-led; the 986/885/866
     counts needing naming where cited).

     Add to the "where the panels and the sentences disagree" list — found this
     session:

     1. "94% of 143" mixes two numbers: 94.4 % is over all 144 pathways, 95.1 %
     over the 143 the
     fits use. Pick one.
     2. "21–27%" is the median per-gene rise across the nuclear compartment
     (+21.4 %) to the mass
     markers (+26.5 %). The set arms drawn in 1E run +20 % to +33 %, so the
     panel's range is
     wider than the sentence's. Either widen the sentence or say which two
     numbers it quotes.
     3. "MYC significantly promoted … OXPHOS subunits (all complexes)" — CIV
     priority is +0.017,
     flat; CV +0.165 and CIII +0.160 carry the tier. And the assembly factors of
     the same
     complexes sit at −0.028. Write subunits, led by CV and CIII.
     4. "glycine cleavage, pyruvate, and serine metabolism" are the top leaves;
     the sentence
     should not also be read as "OXPHOS leads", which is true at tier level
     only.
     5. "Fig. 1G … both in the whole and mitochondrial transcriptome" needs a
     whole-transcriptome
     number. The one that exists in a numbered script is script 44's fitted
     global rate 0.487
     over 8,774 genes (collapse_module_ownership.rds$defs$global_rate_fitted),
     against the
     143-pathway slope 0.552 (R² 0.801) and the OXPHOS tier R² 0.761 content /
     0.945 priority.
     Which pair the sentence quotes is still open — resolve when 1G is built.
     6. Bbc3 is written "(p < 0.01)" — the interaction p is 0.0081 and its BH is
     0.84. The
     licence is pre-specification; the text should say so once (it currently
     does not).
     7. "the overall proliferation signalling remained relatively stable" (2E) —
     right, with one
     qualifier. On the enrichment ruler it is as clean as it gets: not one of 
     the 14
     proliferation sets is significant on 6>12W_wt, median NES −0.89 against the
     TEB
     programmes' −1.56 with 30 of 41 significant. But on the gene-level ruler
     PROLIF_* pooled is
     −0.047 log2 at the 1.3rd percentile of its own matched null (p = 0.013) —
     small, not
     immobile, which is script 43's own phrase. Suggested: keep "relatively
     stable" and add the
     contrast that makes it quantitative (an 8.9-fold smaller move than the TEB
     arm).

     One trap to record: results/mitopps_scores.rds (12:35) is one minute older
     than
     gsva_scores.rds (12:36) although it was regenerated in the same
     post-reconciliation re-run, so
     require_fresher_than() would wrongly stop Fig. 1F. Gate 1F on
     interaction_results.rds
     instead and say why.

     ---
     Verification

     Per panel, before handing over: render through a scratchpad-redirected dry
     run
     (options(myc.fig.nosave = TRUE) + pdf(NULL)) so the whole draw pipeline
     executes and the
     repo tree is untouched; check the stopifnot assertions fire against the
     analysis of record;
     pdftotext the real PDF once written to confirm the base-pdf() device kept
     inter-word
     spacing. Then source(here::here("figures","panels","rebuild_panels.R")) to
     confirm the panel
     joins the set and its legend block lands in legends.md in slot order.

     Working rules that stay in force

     - Option A — I write scripts/ + figures/ + paper/; the author runs them in
     Positron,
     except where the author says "run it".
     - The publication rule — theme_panel() blanks title/subtitle/caption; every
     script carries
     a panel_legend() block; no prose on a panel.
     - Use the declared scheme — group_cols/group_labels, mito_class_*,
     ms_diverging,
     contrast_levels, programme_group(), mouse Myc. Anything new is declared in
     _panel_common.R, never inlined.
     - No new numbered analysis script is implied by any of the 14 panels; all
     read
     results/*.rds already on disk.
     - Git — ./paper/clean_icon_files.sh repo before the first git command
     (Drive mount);
     git mv for renames; leave
     docs/library_reference/gray_chea_mito_tf_shortlist.csv
     untracked; commit per signed-off panel; no force-push.

          +ype gland having changed

          +C-ER animals never saw Myc. It is

          +ng.

          +ll, 2026-08-04) — the same                                    
      596 +instrument as Figs. 1D and 1H, so "enrichment" means one thing
          + across the figure set. **One                                 
      597 +ranked list, so the normaliser is shared**, which is what make
          +s this comparison safe in exactly                             
      598 +the way Fig. 1H's *cross-contrast* comparison was not. No stat
          +ement about the size of an NES                                
      599 +crosses a contrast anywhere on this panel.

      603 +

      608 +| TEB x MitoCarta | 12 | +0.49 | 0 |                          
      609 +| proliferation | 14 | **−0.89** | **0** |                    
      610 +                                                              
      611 +**The `_VS_DUCTAL_*_DN` sets were found inside the TEB row on 
          +the first render and taken out.**                             
      612 +They hold the genes *lower* in the end bud than in the duct, s
          +o their sign is inverted by                                   
      613 +construction and pooling them cancels part of the effect being
          + measured. On their own row they                              
      614 +become the control the sentence needs: *"lost as expected in t
          +he puberty-adult transition"* is a                            
      615 +**directional** claim, and a panel showing only sets going dow
          +n could be showing a ranking that                             
      616 +drifts down. These rise. Two of the three lineages show the re
          +ciprocal outright (basal +1.45                                
      617 +against its UP set's −2.34, hormone-sensing +1.06 against −2.7
          +6); the alveolar pair does not                                
      618 +(−1.13 against −1.22, neither significant). Same role the OXPH
          +OS assembly factors play in                                   
      619 +`figures/fig04_substrate_specificity.R` panel A.              
      620 +                                                              
      621 +**The `*_TEB_MITO` lanes get the treatment Fig. 1D gives the s
          +ame class** — their own row, not                              
      622 +deletion and not dilution. 17–59 genes each, MitoCarta subsets
          + by build, median +0.49: they do                              
      623 +not track the programme they are named for.                   
      624 +                                                              
      625 +**Three rulers agree on the TEB arm and two are independent of

      627 +expression-matched random sets; purity-adjusted per-sample com
          +posite (`score ~ tp * genotype +                              
      628 +epithelial + immune`) **−0.4200, p 0.0053**. They agree to **0
          +.002**, so residual stromal or                                
      629 +immune contamination does not explain the loss. Asserted in th
          +e script, reported in the legend                              
      630 +block, not drawn.                                             
      631 +                                                              
      632 +**Dashed guides at NES = ±1** — the scale of an unmoved set, s
          +ince fGSEA divides the enrichment                             
      633 +score by the mean of the same-signed permutation null. It is t
          +he reference the proliferation row                            
      634 +is read against, and that row's median (−0.89) sits inside it.
      635 +

⏺ Fig. 2E is built — figures/panels/fig2_wt_teb_proliferation.R, 89 × 44 mm, PDF
  written, 14/14 panels rebuild, committed b4c9c73.

  What it draws. Every gene set on one ranked list — the fGSEA NES on 6>12W_wt —
  in four rows:

  ┌───────────────────┬─────┬────────────┬─────────────────────────┐
  │        row        │  n  │ median NES │       padj < 0.05       │
  ├───────────────────┼─────┼────────────┼─────────────────────────┤
  │ TEB programmes    │ 38  │ −1.58      │ 29, and all 38 depleted │
  ├───────────────────┼─────┼────────────┼─────────────────────────┤
  │ ductal (TEB-down) │ 3   │ +1.06      │ 1                       │
  ├───────────────────┼─────┼────────────┼─────────────────────────┤
  │ TEB × MitoCarta   │ 12  │ +0.49      │ 0                       │
  ├───────────────────┼─────┼────────────┼─────────────────────────┤
  │ proliferation     │ 14  │ −0.89      │ 0                       │
  └───────────────────┴─────┴────────────┴─────────────────────────┘

  Shape is significance (Fig. 1F's encoding), the vertical rule is the family
  median, dashed guides at NES ±1 mark the scale of an unmoved set, and
  MG_TEB_VS_DUCTAL_HS_GRAY_UP (−2.76, p 3.4e−18) is labelled because it is the
  one set all three rulers share.

  One thing the first render caught. The _VS_DUCTAL_*_DN sets were sitting
  inside the TEB row with their sign inverted by construction — they hold the
  genes lower in the end bud than in the duct. Pooling them cancelled part of
  the effect. On their own row they become the control your sentence actually
  needs: "lost as expected in the puberty-adult transition" is a directional
  claim, and a panel showing only sets going down could just be showing a
  ranking that drifts down. They rise — basal +1.45 against its UP set's −2.34,
  hormone-sensing +1.06 against −2.76 (alveolar shows neither, −1.13 vs −1.22,
  both n.s.).

  Three things for the text, all in the legend block and PANELS.md:

  1. "Relatively stable" is right, with a qualifier. Not one proliferation set
  is significant here — but on the gene-level ruler PROLIF_* pooled moves −0.047
  log2 at the 1.3rd percentile of its matched null (p = 0.013). Small, not 
  immobile. The quantitative form: the TEB arm moves 8.9 times as far.
  2. Three rulers agree on the TEB arm and two are composition-independent —
  gene-level −0.4220 (0th percentile of 2000 matched sets) and the
  purity-adjusted per-sample composite −0.4200 (p 0.0053), agreeing to 0.002.
  Contamination does not explain the loss.
  3. The cell cycle really does not move — lineage does. Generic E2F1 regulons
  are flat (DOROTHEA −1.18, ChIP-Atlas +0.86, both n.s.) while the Gray _LE
  lanes of the same transcription factors fall hard (FOXM1_HS_LE −2.10,
  TFDP1_HS_LE −2.08, E2F1_HS_LE −1.72, padj < 1e−3). Those are lineage-identity
  lanes. This is Fig. 1H's off-diagonal cloud arriving exactly where section 2
  wants it.

  Next in citation order is Fig. 2F — the respiratory withdrawal. Worth knowing
  before you look at it: on this same NES ruler, MITOCARTA_OXPHOS_SUBUNITS is
  −2.67 (padj 1.3e−11) while amino-acid +1.84 and fatty-acid oxidation +1.89
  rise, and the assembly factors sit at −0.74, n.s. — your sentence's three
  clauses, on one instrument, with its own internal control.

⏺ Stripped to the sentence — 89 × 30 mm, two rows, one axis, one key, committed
  41cd741.

  Everything you named came off: the TEBvsDuctal(HS) label, both median bars,
  the TEB-down row, the TEB×MitoCarta row. You were right about the last one —
  it was answering Fig. 2F's question, not this one.

  What the panel now says on its own: 38 TEB programmes, every one below −1.1,
  29 significant; 14 proliferation sets sitting on the ±1 band, none
  significant. The dashed guides at ±1 turned out to make the median rules
  redundant — that's where a set that hasn't moved lands (fGSEA divides by the
  mean of the same-signed permutation null), so the proliferation row reads as
  "unmoved" directly, and the TEB row's real statement isn't its median but that
  all 38 clear the band.

  The two dropped families are still computed and asserted, and reported in the
  legend block. That distinction is load-bearing in the script: omitting them
  from the page is not the same as folding them into the TEB row, since the _DN
  sets have their sign inverted by construction and the _MITO lanes are
  MitoCarta subsets by build. The script identifies four families and draws two,
  so neither can drift back in.

  The direction control therefore now lives only in the text. If you want a
  stronger verb than "lost", the data support "the gland moved from the end-bud 
  programme toward the ductal one" — basal _DN +1.45 against its _UP set's
  −2.34, hormone-sensing +1.06 against −2.76 (alveolar shows neither). Recorded
  in PANELS.md with the other 2E wording items.

  Ready for Fig. 2F when you are.

      673 +MitoPathway *gains* content over this window (**+0.041, 72 % o

      674 +respiratory arm is not falling with the compartment; it is fal
          +ling against it.                                              
      675 +                                                              
      676 +**The internal control is on the panel, one row below the four
          + complexes:** the assembly factors                            
      677 +*of the same complexes* sit at **+0.001 / −0.002**, percentile
          + **50.2** of script 43's                                      
      678 +expression-matched null, where the pooled subunits sit at **0.
          +0**. What the gland withdraws is the                          
      679 +structural stoichiometry of the chain, not the machinery that 
          +builds it. This is the row that                               
      680 +makes the claim specific rather than a general shrinkage — and
          + it is measured in the same                                   
      681 +libraries on the same two batches, which is the strongest avai
          +lable answer to `batch = timepoint`.                          
      682 +                                                              
      683 +**"Across all complexes" has one exception, and it needs a wor
          +d in the text.** **CII subunits                               
      684 ++0.088 / +0.026** — the only respiratory complex with no mtDNA
          +-encoded subunit, outside the proton                          
      685 +circuit, and also a TCA enzyme. It is the only one that does n
          +ot fall. But **n = 4**, and MitoCarta                         
      686 +sets are membership-loose, so it is a direction to note and no
          +t a mechanism to claim. Suggested:                            
      687 +*"across the four complexes that carry mtDNA-encoded subunits"
          +*, or simply *"CI, CIII, CIV and                              
      688 +CV"*.                                                         
      689 +                                                              
      690 +**The mtDNA-encoded subunits are excluded** by the same `is_mt
          +dna` rule Figs. 1E and 1F use — and                           
      691 +they are the largest movement in the compartment (**+0.633 con
          +tent, +0.525 priority**). That is not                         
      692 +tidiness: the mtDNA-encoded read fraction is confounded three 
          +ways (real content, the proliferation                         
      693 +denominator, dissociation leak) and it is the one quantity tha
          +t is **time-associated rather than                            
      694 +genotype-associated**, so a temporal contrast is exactly where
          + it cannot be read. A nuclear-down /                          
      695 +mtDNA-up mitonuclear discordance is what it looks like; on thi
          +s axis it is not adjudicable. Stated                          
      696 +in the legend block so the omission is visible.               
      697 +                                                              
      698 +**No significance is drawn and none exists** for this contrast
          + — script 40 saves adjusted p-values                          
      699 +for the genotype contrasts only. What exists is script 43's ex
          +pression-matched null, which covers                           
      700 +five of the eleven rows and is quoted in the legend. Within-co
          +mpartment percentiles are a *rank                             
      701 +among MitoPathways*, not a test, and are labelled as such.    
      702 +                                                              
      703 +**Scripts 40 and 43 computed the wild-type arms independently 
          +and the script asserts they agree**                           
      704 +to 1e−6, via `$defs$arms` (script 43's own arm→pathway map) — 
          +an identity check across two                                  
      705 +analyses, not a re-derivation in the figure layer.            
      706 +

          +e panel edge** (the trap Fig. 1E hit

⏺ Fig. 2F is built — figures/panels/fig2_wt_mito_contraction.R, 89 × 56 mm,
  15/15 panels rebuild, committed adb4e94.

  Eleven MitoPathways ranked on the wild-type 6→12W contrast, drawn on both 
  rulers side by side — content (set-average raw log2FC) and priority (mitoPPS,
  content-blind) — on separate scales, because they are separate units. Reading
  top to bottom:

  ┌───────────────────────────────┬────────────────────┬────────────────────┐
  │                               │      content       │      priority      │
  ├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ CIV subunits (17)             │ −0.411             │ −0.180             │
  ├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ CI (36) / CIII (9) / CV (19)  │ −0.253 / −0.251 /  │ −0.155 / −0.126 /  │
  │ subunits                      │ −0.216             │ −0.069             │
  ├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ mitoribosome (83), central    │ −0.024, +0.036     │ −0.041, −0.011     │
  │ dogma (230)                   │                    │                    │
  ├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ OXPHOS assembly factors (66)  │ +0.001             │ −0.002             │
  ├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ CII subunits (4)              │ +0.088             │ +0.026             │
  ├───────────────────────────────┼────────────────────┼────────────────────┤
  │ lipid (111), amino acid (79), │ +0.122 … +0.227    │ +0.063 … +0.144    │
  │  FAO (39)                     │                    │                    │
  └───────────────────────────────┴────────────────────┴────────────────────┘

  Both rulers are drawn because either alone is answerable — a content drop
  could be normalisation, a content-blind ratio drop could be a reshuffle inside
  a growing compartment. Over all 143 non-mtDNA pathways they agree at Spearman
  0.82.

  Two things came out of the data that strengthen your sentence:

  1. The background is rising. The median MitoPathway gains content over this
  window (+0.041, 72% above zero). So the respiratory arm isn't falling with the
  compartment — it's falling against it. That is what licenses "withdraw", and
  it's one clause.
  2. The assembly-factor control is doing more work than I expected. They sit at
  +0.001, percentile 50.2 of the expression-matched null, where the pooled
  subunits sit at 0.0 — measured in the same libraries, on the same two batches.
  That's the strongest available answer to batch = timepoint, and it belongs in
  the text: the gland withdraws the stoichiometry of the chain, not the 
  machinery that builds it.

  One wording item. "Across all complexes" has an exception: CII subunits rise
  (+0.088 / +0.026) — the only complex with no mtDNA-encoded subunit and the
  only one outside the proton circuit. But n = 4, so it's a direction, not a
  mechanism. Suggested: "across the four complexes that carry mtDNA-encoded 
  subunits", or just name CI, CIII, CIV, CV.

  And one deliberate omission, flagged in the legend rather than hidden. The 13
  mtDNA-encoded subunits are excluded by the same is_mtdna rule as Figs. 1E/1F —
  and they are the largest movement in the compartment (+0.633 content, +0.525 
  priority). That's a nuclear-down/mtDNA-up discordance if real, but mt% is
  confounded three ways and is time-associated rather than genotype-associated,
  so the wild-type temporal contrast is precisely where it can't be read. The
  text shouldn't quote it either.

  No significance is drawn because none exists for this contrast — script 40
  saves adjusted p-values for the genotype contrasts only. What does exist is
  script 43's matched null, covering five of the eleven rows, and it's in the
  legend. Scripts 40 and 43 computed these arms independently; the script
  asserts they agree to 1e−6.

  Next is S2B — the WT temporal programme's independence from the Myc
  reallocation (the r = −0.02).

✻ Cooked for 9m 19s

  x, mitoPPS priority on y, all 143 non-mtDNA MitoPathways on the plane, filled
  on the ms_diverging ramp (espresso down / white at zero / mint up). No colour
  bar: the fill repeats the x axis, so the axis is the key.

  You were right that this works better — the plane says three things the two
  ranked columns couldn't:

  - the cloud runs along a diagonal (Spearman 0.82), so the two rulers agree and
  the lower-left quadrant is the conjunction;
  - its centre sits up and right of the origin — the compartment gains content
  over this window (median +0.041, 72% above zero), so the chain falls against a
  rising background, which is what licenses "withdraw";
  - the OXPHOS assembly factors sit at the origin, a few millimetres from their 
  own subunits — that distance is the whole specificity claim, and now you can
  see it rather than read it.

  Significance — yes, three kinds, and one of them I hadn't surfaced

  1. Per pathway, priority ruler. Script 08 does test this contrast
  (Temporal_Myc-; its diff is asserted identical to the ruler's p_tn). 0 of 143 
  clear BH, smallest padj 0.12. But 23 clear raw p = 0.05 against 7.2 expected, 
  binomial p = 8e−7 — the compartment moves coherently and no single pathway
  carries it.
  2. Per pathway, content ruler. Nothing saved, and I deliberately didn't
  compute a one-sample t over member genes: that null ignores inter-gene
  correlation and would be anti-conservative for exactly the
  coherently-regulated sets this panel is about.
  3. Arm level, expression-matched null (2000 draws) — decisive: OXPHOS subunits
  percentile 0.0 (p < 0.0005) against their own assembly factors at 50.2;
  amino-acid 100.0 and lipid 99.9 upward.

  The number to put in the sentence is one I hadn't given you: script 43's 
  paired null. "The chain falls while biogenesis does not" is a comparative
  claim, and script 43 tests it by redrawing both sets together — OXPHOS 
  subunits minus mitoribosome = −0.230, percentile 0.05, p = 0.0005 (minus
  nucleotide metabolism −0.255, p = 0.0085; minus the pooled proliferation set
  −0.208, p < 0.0005). That is the statistic for this sentence, and it's a
  stronger one than either arm alone.

  Nothing is drawn, because a glyph would have to mark 8 tested pathways among
  135 untested ones — which reads as 135 negatives. All of it is in the legend
  block.

  Two things worth knowing about the build. Labels are placed, not repelled —
  ggrepel stacked the four knot labels on each other twice, so they go into two
  provably empty regions (upper-left, and the right band past +0.28), with the
  emptiness re-derived from the data in a stopifnot rather than trusted from the
  render, and lane order matched to the points' vertical order so no two
  leaders can cross. And the panel is windowed — two pathways sit below the
  priority range, both ≤5 genes. That's not incidental: every point at the 
  periphery of this cloud is a 3-to-6-gene set, because a set mean's noise
  scales as 1/√n. The membership-loose caveat, made visible.

  Next is S2B — the WT temporal programme's independence from the Myc
  reallocation.

⏺ Both done — committed a108d0e.

  Leaders now start on the label's line for all eleven points. That meant
  dropping ggrepel from this panel entirely: it draws from wherever the box edge
  falls, so a repelled label and a placed one don't read as the same object.
  The four respiratory complexes joined the other seven, in a bottom band the
  script proves is empty. Three lanes now — upper-left (the knot), bottom (the
  complexes), right (the catabolic three).

  The swap is in, and it exposed a real flaw in what I had. My non-crossing
  guarantee was the heuristic "lane order = the points' own vertical order" —
  which would have forbidden your swap, because OXPHOS assembly sits above
  central dogma in y. But its point lies further left, and that's what actually
  decides: the higher label reaching further left is exactly what crossed the
  two leaders. Ordering by y was the wrong invariant. It's replaced by the real
  test — an orientation check over all 55 pairs of leaders, asserted in the
  script, so any future re-lane fails loudly instead of quietly crossing.

  Label-box emptiness is checked the same way: in_box() over boxes covering the
  text extent, re-derived from the data rather than trusted from a render.

  Next is S2B — "the WT temporal program operated independently of the
  MYC-driven reallocation, showing a negligible correlation across the
  mitochondrial and the whole transcriptome" (the r = −0.02).

  ⎿  68d45a6 negligible, once the two contrasts stop sharing six animals

⏺ Fig. S2B is built — figS2_reallocation_independence.R, 89 × 52 mm, 16/16
  panels rebuild, committed 68d45a6.

  Two scatters of the wild-type 6→12W change against the Myc effect at 6W: 143 
  MitoPathways on the priority ruler (r = −0.022) and 15,191 genes on the log2 
  fold change (r = +0.228).

  The mitochondrial half is exactly as you wrote it. The whole-transcriptome 
  half needed work, and the finding matters.

  myc_6W = 6W_myc − 6W_wt and 6>12W_wt = 12W_wt − 6W_wt — the same six wild-type
  animals are subtracted in both, so a fluctuation in that baseline moves the
  two contrasts together and manufactures a positive correlation. Two
  independent estimates:

  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┬────────┐
  │                                                                 │        │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────┤
  │ observed                                                        │ +0.228 │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────┤
  │ predicted from the standard errors (Cov = Var(6W_wt) = SE²/2)   │ +0.198 │
  ├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┼────────┤
  │ measured — Myc effect against one half of the six animals,      │ −0.025 │
  │ temporal effect against the other                               │        │
  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┴────────┘

  So the sentence holds on both scopes: −0.02 mitochondrial, −0.03 whole 
  transcriptome. The dashed line on the right-hand panel is what the tilt
  becomes once the baseline is broken; the solid line is the observed one.

  Three things about the control worth knowing. It is exhaustive, not sampled —
  there are exactly choose(6,3) = 20 assignments and all 20 are used, so the
  panel contains no RNG. It runs on VST rather than re-fitted DESeq2 models
  (re-fitting is an analysis, not a figure), and what licenses that surrogate is
  asserted: with the baseline shared, the VST version reproduces the drawn
  DESeq2 correlation to 0.002. And splitting halves the baseline's n, which
  pulls |r| toward zero — undoing that inflation gives −0.032 rather than
  −0.025, so it doesn't turn on which you use.

  The artefact also pushes the wrong way for the mitochondrial panel, which
  makes that negative conservative: the priority contrasts share the same
  baseline, so the sharing forces a positive correlation, and −0.02 is what we
  see.

  Two things for the record, both in PANELS.md:

  - Cosine is not correlation here. Script 40 reports the uncentred cosine
  (+0.210 on content); the correlation is −0.038. Both content vectors have a
  large positive mean and the cosine picks up that shared offset. The script
  asserts its recomputation matches $geometry exactly, so the panel provably
  draws script 40's vectors — but your sentence says correlation, so correlation
  is what is drawn. (Script 40's own null agrees: the cosine sits at the 41st
  percentile, i.e. at chance.)
  - This is not the claim that the Myc+ gland's own drift is independent of the 
  wild-type one. That regression has slope 0.749 — much of it is shared.
  Different pairs of vectors; they mustn't be run together in the text.

  Next is S2C — "the overall apoptotic priming remains stable in the WT
  timeline".

  ⎿  fd05adc one of thirty-seven moves, and it moves the wrong way

⏺ Fig. S2C is built — figS2_priming_balance.R, 89 × 38 mm, 17/17 panels rebuild,
  committed fd05adc. It's the port of fig04 panel D: 25 pro- and 7
  anti-apoptotic MitoCarta transcripts plus the 5 non-MitoCarta brakes as their
  own row.

  One of 37 moves, and it moves the wrong way for a loss of priming — Bnip3
  +0.63, padj 0.021, a pro-apoptotic gene going up. Nothing else clears 0.05 on
  either arm or among the brakes.

  "Overall priming" is a balance, and both arms move together — so the balance 
  doesn't move. On script 40's ruler (the same instrument as 2F): pro +0.025 vs
  anti +0.045 on content (difference −0.020), and +0.054 vs +0.046 on priority
  (+0.008). For contrast, Myc moves the same two arms apart: +0.156 against
  −0.024, a difference of +0.180 — an order of magnitude larger and in the
  opposite geometry. The balance is something Myc changes and development does 
  not. That sentence may be worth having in the text; it makes the negative do
  work rather than just sit there.

  The negative has its power control. On the same transcripts, in the same
  libraries, at the same n, the genotype contrast reaches padj < 0.05 for 6 of 
  35 mapped genes against 1 across the window. (The symbol→Ensembl mapping that
  needs is checked against baseMean rather than trusted.)

  Three genes labelled because later panels turn on them: Bnip3, Bbc3 (PUMA) at
  +0.062, padj 0.88 — flat across the window, which is what Fig. 2H needs — and
  Bcl2l1 (Bcl-xL) at −0.107, padj 0.79, the denominator of Fig. 2G's ratio.

  Two things I put in the legend explicitly rather than leaving to be inferred:

  - Script 34's circularity warning does not transfer here. The PRO/ANTI sets
  are MitoCarta, so a coupling between priming and a mito axis is mito-vs-mito —
  but this is a temporal contrast on the transcripts themselves, correlated
  with nothing. Easy to import the caveat wrongly.
  - A negative on transcripts is not a negative on priming. Priming is the
  protein complement and the distance to threshold; transcripts are its
  substrate. BH3 profiling is the measurement, and this panel is a reason to do
  it rather than a substitute.

  One build note: vertical offsets are a deterministic ladder, not jitter —
  which is what let the three labels be placed at known coordinates instead of
  repelled, into two regions the script asserts are empty.

  Next is 2G — "most pro-/anti-apoptotic ratios established by MYC at 6W are
  reduced according to the global rescaling … the PUMA/BCL-XL ratio exhibited a
  significant reversal."

  ├─────────────────┼────────┼───────────┤

  ├─────────────────┼────────┼───────────┤
  │ Bcl2l11 (BIM)   │ +0.255 │ 0.37      │
  ├─────────────────┼────────┼───────────┤
  │ Bbc3 (PUMA)     │ +0.062 │ 0.88      │
  ├─────────────────┼────────┼───────────┤
  │ Bnip3           │ +0.635 │ 0.021     │
  ├─────────────────┼────────┼───────────┤
  │ Bcl2 (BCL-2)    │ +0.461 │ 0.45      │

  └─────────────────┴────────┴───────────┘

  Only Bnip3 clears 0.05 — which is the panel's point, and it now reads with the
  named genes rather than despite them. The legend glosses every symbol with
  the protein name the text uses.

  Seven labels don't fit beside their own points, so they take four lanes: a
  reserved lane above the pro row; the gap between the pro and anti rows for
  Bak1 (0.039 from Bbc3 in x — too close to share the top lane); inline to the
  right for Bcl2, which is the rightmost point of its row; and out to the left
  for Bcl2l1.

  Every position is checked rather than nudged — a label half-width in x units
  is derived from the drawn text size, and stopifnot requires that no two
  top-lane labels touch, that the rightmost stays inside the panel, that the
  left lane is clear of data, and that the inline label really belongs to its
  row's extreme point. If the data shift under it, the panel stops instead of
  overprinting.

⏺ Fig. 2G is built to the corrected sentence — fig2_priming_ratios.R, 89 × 58
  mm, 18/18 panels rebuild, committed 0c94f28.

  Your correction is the one the data support, and it made the panel simpler.
  It's Fig. 1G's axes one level up: the Myc effect on each pro-apoptotic:Bcl-xL
  log₂ ratio at 12W against 6W, with the global rescaling rate 0.487 drawn as
  the expected pattern (labelled x 0.49 on the page, since a bare line through a
  scatter reads as a regression and this one is imposed).

  ┌───────┬────────┬────────┬───────────┬────────────────┐
  │ ratio │   6W   │  12W   │ retention │                │
  ├───────┼────────┼────────┼───────────┼────────────────┤
  │ Bax   │ +0.901 │ +0.495 │ 0.55      │ on the line    │
  ├───────┼────────┼────────┼───────────┼────────────────┤
  │ Bid   │ +0.946 │ +0.396 │ 0.42      │ on the line    │
  ├───────┼────────┼────────┼───────────┼────────────────┤
  │ Bak1  │ +0.688 │ +0.185 │ 0.27      │ a little under │
  ├───────┼────────┼────────┼───────────┼────────────────┤
  │ Bbc3  │ +0.674 │ −0.061 │ −0.09     │ crosses zero   │
  └───────┴────────┴────────┴───────────┴────────────────┘

  The members clause is exact, and it's in the legend: Bax retains 0.52 of its
  6W effect and Bcl2l1 0.57 — both at the global 0.49 — so their ratio retains
  0.55. Bbc3 retains −1.10 against that same denominator. That is the whole of
  the difference, and it's why "remained stable" is the right verb for the
  others.

  One shared denominator is what makes the comparison internal (script 42's own
  design note): BAX priming retains the global rate while PUMA priming reverses
  against the same Bcl-xL, so "it's just the global attenuation" is refuted from
  inside the panel.

  Shape is "established at 6W" and it's load-bearing, not decorative. Retention
  is a quotient — for the three pairs whose 6W effect is indistinguishable from
  zero it carries no information (Pmaip1's retention is +2.08 purely because its
  denominator is near zero). They're open circles with no residual drawn, and
  the four that are drawn are exactly the scope your sentence names.

  One item for the text: "significantly" needs its scope. The interaction is p =
  0.037 raw, 0.017 purity-adjusted — but BH across the nine pairs is 0.33, and
  against the matched-pair conditional null the empirical p is 0.082. It's a
  nominal result with a pre-specified licence (PUMA was named in advance from
  the PGC1a work), not a multiplicity-surviving one. The counterweight is in the
  legend too: on the empirical null the most extreme pair is Bmf, which Myc
  never established — which is exactly why the conditional null is the one to
  read.

  And a caution worth carrying: the gene-level Bbc3 interaction (p = 0.0081,
  which is 2H's subject) is a different statistic from this ratio interaction (p
  = 0.037). Easy to interchange.

  Next is 2H — Foxo3 tracking Bbc3 in the departure-from-dose scan.

⏺ Committed f61ae42.

  The line's label now lies along the line. The underlying problem was that a
  horizontal caption cannot sit parallel to a sloped one — over the width of the
  text the line climbs further than the text is tall, so one end always drifts.
  That's what "slipped too high" was. It's rotated to the line's angle on the 
  page (≈22°), computed from the panel's aspect rather than guessed. The two
  plot-area constants are measured for this panel size and the script says so,
  because the clearance assertion catches a label that has drifted off the line
  but not one drawn at the wrong angle. The clearance check itself is a
  perpendicular distance, so it doesn't depend on the rotation being exact.

  The key moved to the bottom-right wedge — where the rising line leaves the one
  region no point and neither reference line can reach. That also meant moving
  Bbc3's label from below its point to its left, which reads better anyway.

  Next is 2H — Foxo3 tracking Bbc3 in the departure-from-dose scan.

  ⎿  4a57c22 four genes of 8,774 lie between them

⏺ Fig. 2H is built — fig2_departure_from_dose.R, 89 × 44 mm, 19/19 panels
  rebuild, committed 4a57c22.

  Script 44's per-gene departure from the dose expectation, drawn as the
  distribution over 8,774 genes with a rug of the bottom percentile:

  ┌─────────┬───────┬─────────────┬────────────┐
  │         │   z   │ genes below │ percentile │
  ├─────────┼───────┼─────────────┼────────────┤
  │ Foxo3   │ −2.53 │ 39 of 8,774 │ 0.46       │
  ├─────────┼───────┼─────────────┼────────────┤
  │ Bbc3    │ −2.49 │ 44          │ 0.51       │
  ├─────────┼───────┼─────────────┼────────────┤
  │ Bcl2l11 │ +1.01 │ 8,036       │ 91.6       │
  └─────────┴───────┴─────────────┴────────────┘

  "Closely tracking" has a sharp form, and the script asserts it: only four 
  genes of 8,774 lie between them. That's the number for the text.

  The panel carries the half that failed, which is why Bcl2l11 is marked. The
  scan was pre-registered with two genes and they split — Bbc3 collapses,
  Bcl2l11 sits in the ordinary middle at 91.6 with an interaction p of 0.86.
  Drawing only the gene that worked would be the wrong panel, so the key
  distinguishes pre-specified (filled) from found in the scan (open).

  And Foxo3 was not pre-specified — it was found here. Forty-four genes sit
  below the 0.5th percentile with it, mostly unrelated (Adh7, Cntnap2, Inhba,
  Wfdc2, Vtcn1…), so position alone is not evidence. What makes it a lead is the
  prior — FOXO3 is PUMA's canonical p53-independent activator with direct ChIP
  evidence. The text should introduce it that way round, and the legend says so.

  Two things I've recorded that may be worth a clause each:

  - The same departure is reached by two different routes. The wild-type gland
  raises Foxo3 with age (+0.473) and the Myc+ gland does not (−0.013); Bbc3's
  wild-type arm is flat (+0.062) while its Myc+ arm falls (−0.479). One is a
  failure to follow the normal developmental rise, the other an active loss.
  - The arrow is not the obvious one. PUMA restrains the mitochondrial pyruvate
  carrier (Kim, Cancer Cell 2019), so "PUMA falls → respiration falls" is
  backwards; respiration is upstream and FOXO3 → BBC3 closes it into feedback
  rather than a chain. Nothing on this panel establishes direction.

  None of the interactions survives correction (Bbc3 BH 0.84, Foxo3 BH 1.00) —
  expected at n = 6 per cell, and why the licence is pre-specification rather
  than the p-value. A z of −2.5 among 8,774 genes is a rank statement, not a
  test.

  Two remain: S2D (the other PUMA inducers) and 2I (the OXPHOS × PUMA:Bcl-xL
  coupling).

⏺ All done. Here's the state and the resume prompt.

  All fourteen panels are built. 21 panel scripts, 21/21 rebuilding clean, 26
  commits local on paper-figures (unpushed, origin still at b0feba3).

  The last two, run here rather than in Positron:

  Fig. S2D — Fig. 2H's objection tested: that some other upstream input to PUMA
  moved and Foxo3 is a bystander. Script 42's own twelve-gene roster, Myc effect
  at each age. Nothing reaches padj < 0.05 at either age and no interaction 
  survives. Foxo3 is in the roster and drawn in bold — it is the one gene that
  isn't "other", so the text should name it as the exception rather than let the
  reader assume the roster excludes it. The other two FOXO paralogues do
  nothing, so it isn't a family effect. Trp73 does move (+2.14 across the
  wild-type window, padj 0.004) on a mean of 12 counts — named in the legend so
  the panel isn't read as flatter than it is.

  Fig. 2I — what a genotype × coupling interaction actually is: two slopes that
  differ. On OXPHOS subunits the genotypes couple in opposite directions (−4.80
  vs +4.35); on redox they're parallel. Redox is the right control because it
  isn't a null axis — both genotypes couple to it strongly, so the interaction
  can't be the ratio tracking mitochondrial scores in general. Two things for
  the text: "other redox and metabolic axes" is one comparator, not several
  (script 43 carries exactly two axes), and the panel is a reconstruction that
  reproduces script 43's interaction to 1.2 % — quote script 43's number, not 
  the panel's. It's a lead: the permutation null puts it at the 91.7th
  percentile (p = 0.083), and a timepoint term takes p from 0.0052 to 0.088.

  Also delivered: Figure2_and_S2_panels.pdf (8 pages: 2E, 2F, 2G, 2H, 2I, S2B,
  S2C, S2D, verified page by page) alongside the existing 12-page Figure 1
  document — one source(panels_to_pdf.R) now writes both, and which document a

     ROUND 2 (2026-08-09) — striking alternatives for Figure 2

     Context

     All fourteen commissioned panels are built and the section is written. The 
     criticism is that the
     Figure 2 panels are hard to read: they are correct but they look like
     supplementary material —
     each one answers its own sentence carefully and none of them carries the
     argument on its own. The
     author's requirement: "the figures have to talk by themselves, people do 
     not read the text."

     The one message Figure 2 must deliver, in the author's words:

     ▎ A complex interplay between the WT and Myc-driven timelines (6→12W) leads
     ▎ to OXPHOS subunit
     ▎ repression, which is more than the overall MYC effect decline. That 
     ▎ OXPHOS decline — with the
     ▎ other metabolic/catabolic rearrangements — is closely coupled to the 
     ▎ selective loss of the
     ▎ PUMA/Bcl-xL priming ratio, presumably driven by the p53-independent PUMA 
     ▎ regulator Foxo3.
     ▎ The lost PUMA trigger is what leaves the adult (12W) gland unable to die,
     ▎ tilting
     ▎ proliferation/apoptosis toward tumour expansion.

     The reworded Doc section (read 2026-08-09) now states this explicitly, and
     it puts the two
     timelines side by side in every claim — which is exactly what the current
     panels do not do:

     ┌───────────────────────────────┬──────────────────────┬──────────────────┐
     │  claim in the reworded text   │        number        │  current panel   │
     │                               │                      │      shows       │
     ├───────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────┼──────────────────┤
     │ OXPHOS falls in the Myc       │ WT −0.255, Myc+      │ only the WT      │
     │ timeline and development adds │ −0.405 (exactly      │ timeline         │
     │  more                         │ additive)            │                  │
     ├───────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────┼──────────────────┤
     │ Bbc3 flat in WT, lost under   │ +0.062 (ns) →        │ only the ratio,  │
     │ Myc                           │ −0.479, padj 0.016   │ only the         │
     │                               │                      │ genotype axis    │
     ├───────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────┼──────────────────┤
     │ Foxo3 rises in WT, falls      │ +0.473 → −0.013;     │ a percentile     │
     │ under Myc                     │ interaction p 0.0074 │ among 8,774      │
     │                               │                      │ genes            │
     ├───────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────┼──────────────────┤
     │ lowest interaction p "among   │ Foxo3 rank 1 of 26,  │                  │
     │ biogenesis/cell-death genes"  │ Bbc3 rank 2, gap to  │ not drawn        │
     │                               │ 3rd (0.066)          │                  │
     ├───────────────────────────────┼──────────────────────┼──────────────────┤
     │ the p53 arm is not what moves │ 9 genes, nothing on  │ S2D draws a      │
     │  PUMA                         │ either timeline      │ different roster │
     └───────────────────────────────┴──────────────────────┴──────────────────┘

     So: keep every current panel, and add a parallel set of alternatives built 
     on one grammar. The
     author picks per slot.

     The grammar (author's choice, 2026-08-09): the two timelines

     Every alternative uses the same axes — x = 6>12W_wt, y = 6>12W_myc — with
     the identity line
     y = x meaning "development alone". Quadrants gain a fixed meaning the
     reader learns once:

     - below the line = the Myc+ gland loses MORE than development alone → the
     Myc-specific term.
     93 % of the 143 MitoPathways sit below it on content, so this is the
     compartment's default.
     - lower left = down in both. On content this is where the OXPHOS arm sits
     and almost nothing
     else.
     - right of zero, below zero = up in development, down under Myc.

     c_tp = c_tn + c_int holds exactly (max deviation 0), and likewise on the
     priority ruler, so a
     vertical drop from the identity line to a point IS the interaction. That is
     the device that makes
     "more than the overall MYC effect decline" visible instead of asserted.

     This grammar already exists in the repo and should be ported rather than
     invented:
     figures/figS7_reallocation_temporal_divergence.R:56-85 draws WT-vs-Myc+
     with the y=x diagonal,
     a rotated diagonal label and a "below the line" quadrant annotation, tier
     colours and repelled
     anchors. figures/figS6_reallocation_temporal_ranked.R is the ranked form of
     the same comparison.

     Making the panels "talk" within the publication rule. theme_panel() still
     blanks
     title/subtitle/caption. What carries the meaning instead is in-panel naming
     of drawn elements —
     the identity line labelled "development alone", a quadrant labelled "lost 
     under Myc only" — the
     same device Fig. 2G already uses for its global rescaling (slope 0.49)
     line. Naming a drawn
     element is what a key does; it is not explanatory prose.

     The four new scripts

     All in figures/panels/, all *_alt.R, all reading results/*.rds only (no new
     analysis).

     1. fig2_two_timelines_mito_alt.R — slot Fig. 2F (alt)
     Two facets sharing the grammar, because the sentence names both rulers
     ("All OXPHOS subunit logFC
     and MitoPPS dropped"): content (set-average raw log2FC) and priority 
     (mitoPPS), 143
     MitoPathways each, x = c_tn/p_tn, y = c_tp/p_tp. Identity line; vertical 
     arrows from the line
     down to the ~11 labelled arms = the Myc-specific extra loss. Labelled:
     CI/CIII/CIV/CV subunits,
     OXPHOS assembly factors, mitoribosome, central dogma, fatty acid oxidation,
     carnitine shuttle,
     glycine cleavage, amino acid, lipid. Source background_vs_myc.rds$ruler.
     Reads as: development raises the compartment (median +0.041, 72 % up) but
     strips OXPHOS; the Myc+
     gland lowers everything, and OXPHOS deepest of all because the two terms
     add.

     2. fig2_death_two_timelines_alt.R — slot Fig. 2G (alt)
     Same axes, per gene, for the death machinery
     (priming_arm_teb.rds$machinery, the BH3-only
     triggers + effectors + brakes). Bbc3 (+0.06 → −0.48) sits alone in "flat in
     development, lost
     under Myc"; Bcl2l1 at the origin; Bmf (+1.02 → +0.84) on the line, i.e.
     purely
     developmental; Bax down in both. Shape = padj < 0.05 on the Myc timeline.
     Reads as: the loss is specific to PUMA, and Bcl-xL does not move — so the
     ratio's reversal is all
     numerator.

     3. fig2_puma_chain_alt.R — slot Fig. 2H+I (alt) (author chose the merge)
     Two parts.
     A — the chain, animal by animal. A per-animal heatmap, 24 columns grouped
     6W_wt | 12W_wt | 6W_myc | 12W_myc, rows = OXPHOS-subunit mitoPPS priority,
     Foxo3, Bbc3, Bcl2l1,
     PUMA:Bcl-xL — row-z, ms_diverging fill. The 12W_myc block falls together;
     that is the mechanism
     made visible without a single p-value. Sources:
     priming_arm_teb.rds$axis_scores,
     gsva_scores.rds$expr_mat.
     B — the coupling. The genotype-split OXPHOS→PUMA:Bcl-xL relationship (WT
     slope −4.80, Myc+
     +4.35, script 43's interaction +6.09, p = 0.0052), i.e. the current 2I's
     content, redrawn
     beneath its own mechanism. Reuse the validated reconstruction in
     figures/panels/fig2_oxphos_puma_coupling.R:89-111 verbatim (it reproduces
     script 43 to 1.2 %).
     Legend carries the Foxo3 rank-1 / Bbc3 rank-2 of 26 mechanism genes by
     interaction p.

     4. figS2_p53_arm_alt.R — slot Fig. S2D (alt)
     Same axes, drawing priming_arm_teb.rds$exclusions$p53_axis (Trp53, Mdm2,
     Cdkn1a, Cdkn2a,
     Trp53inp1, Zmat3, Eda2r, Phlda3, Ccng1) — the author's point that the 
     p53-dependent arm does not
     move — with exclusions$puma_inputs as a second, lighter class and Bbc3 and 
     Foxo3 plotted as
     the two reference points that do. Nothing in the p53 arm has an
     interaction; Trp53 itself is
     flat on every contrast.
     Reads as: PUMA is lost without p53 moving, which is what makes Foxo3 the
     candidate.

     One shared addition to figures/panels/_panel_common.R

     two_timeline_base(lim, label_diag = TRUE, label_quadrant = NULL) — returns
     the list of layers
     every alternative starts from: zero lines, the y = x identity line, its
     rotated label
     ("development alone"), and optional quadrant text. Declared once so the
     four panels cannot drift
     apart, exactly as bracket_frame()/heat_fill() are. Nothing else new:
     ms_diverging,
     group_cols, sig_cols, lab_signed, save_panel_p, panel_legend all already
     cover the rest.

     Where they go

     rebuild_panels.R picks up fig*.R automatically. panels_to_pdf.R gains a
     third document,
     Figure2_alternatives.pdf, matched on the (alt) slot suffix, and the two
     existing documents get
     the suffix excluded from their regexes so the current circulation PDFs are
     unchanged. The
     author then has Figure2_and_S2_panels.pdf and Figure2_alternatives.pdf side
     by side.

     Text items this round has already turned up

     - Foxo3 is rank 1 and Bbc3 rank 2 of the 26 biogenesis/cell-death mechanism
     genes by
     interaction p (0.0074, 0.0081) with a clear gap to the third (Pgs1, 0.066).
     This is a far
     stronger and more legible framing than the 8,774-gene percentile the
     current 2H uses, and it is
     what the reworded text already claims.
     - Bmf rises in BOTH timelines (+1.02 WT padj 0.0002, +0.84 Myc+ padj 0.003)
     — the one death
     gene that is purely developmental. It belongs in the text as the contrast
     to Bbc3.
     - Bax also falls significantly on the Myc timeline (−0.376, padj 0.0077).
     "Specific to
     Bbc3" holds among BH3-only sensors; Bax is an effector, and the sentence
     should say so.
     - Trp73 moves on the wild-type timeline (+2.14, padj 0.004) on 12 counts —
     floor of the assay.

     ---
     What the six built panels changed in the text (all recorded in PANELS.md)

     Each build checked its sentence against the data first. Four sentences need
     a word changed and one
     needs rewriting — none of it changes a result.

     panel: 1E
     what it found: "94% of 143" mixes the 144-row and 143-row fractions;
     "21–27%"
     is narrower than the arms drawn (+20 to +33%); the padj < 0.001 is a pooled

     genotype effect and only biogenesis clears 0.05 at six weeks alone
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     panel: 1F
     what it found: "significantly promoted … OXPHOS subunits (all complexes)" 
     fails
     — OXPHOS subunits padj 0.27 and not one complex clears 0.05; the only
     significant OXPHOS row is CIII assembly factors at −0.141, demoted. Glycine

     cleavage (0.118) and mitophagy (0.084) also miss
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     panel: 1G
     what it found: the R2=*** placeholders are filled — and the p attaches to 
     the 
     R², not the slope (64 of 500 shuffles reach the slope, none reach the R²)
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     panel: 1H
     what it found: "enhanced" does not survive. The rise is global (81% of all
     sets, 93% of the enriched), the two named families rise by the median
     amount,
      and a global NES rise is what a flatter 12W ranking mechanically produces.

     What is true: they hold their position while the amplitude halves
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     panel: S1E
     what it found: "the expression gap remained constant between 6W_myc and 
     12W_myc" — a gap is between genotypes, not ages; both readings are true and

     the panel draws both, but say which
     ────────────────────────────────────────
     panel: S1F
     what it found: clean negative; nothing to change

     Also delivered

     figures/panels/panels_to_pdf.R →
     outputs/figures/panels/Figure1_and_S1_panels.pdf, twelve
     pages, one panel each at true size, slot as the page title, for circulating
     beside the Doc text.

     PANELS.md — DONE, and kept current per panel

     Rewritten 2026-08-04 and extended with a signed-off entry per panel. The
     original brief was:
     replace the "Planned — Figures 2 and 3 (Order D)" section with the map
     above;
     move the built rows into a Figure-1 / Supplementary-1 table that carries
     the written
     sentences, not the pre-writing ones; move figS1_mb_fork_specificity.R to a
     "built, no slot
     yet" row; and carry forward the three unresolved text items (1D tier order
     E2F +2.08 above
     biosynthetic metabolism +1.84; "aligned almost entirely" →
     mitochondria-led; the 986/885/866
     counts needing naming where cited).

     Add to the "where the panels and the sentences disagree" list — found this
     session:

     1. "94% of 143" mixes two numbers: 94.4 % is over all 144 pathways, 95.1 %
     over the 143 the
     fits use. Pick one.
     2. "21–27%" is the median per-gene rise across the nuclear compartment
     (+21.4 %) to the mass
     markers (+26.5 %). The set arms drawn in 1E run +20 % to +33 %, so the
     panel's range is
     wider than the sentence's. Either widen the sentence or say which two
     numbers it quotes.
     3. "MYC significantly promoted … OXPHOS subunits (all complexes)" — CIV
     priority is +0.017,
     flat; CV +0.165 and CIII +0.160 carry the tier. And the assembly factors of
     the same
     complexes sit at −0.028. Write subunits, led by CV and CIII.
     4. "glycine cleavage, pyruvate, and serine metabolism" are the top leaves;
     the sentence
     should not also be read as "OXPHOS leads", which is true at tier level
     only.
     5. "Fig. 1G … both in the whole and mitochondrial transcriptome" needs a
     whole-transcriptome
     number. The one that exists in a numbered script is script 44's fitted
     global rate 0.487
     over 8,774 genes (collapse_module_ownership.rds$defs$global_rate_fitted),
     against the
     143-pathway slope 0.552 (R² 0.801) and the OXPHOS tier R² 0.761 content /
     0.945 priority.
     Which pair the sentence quotes is still open — resolve when 1G is built.
     6. Bbc3 is written "(p < 0.01)" — the interaction p is 0.0081 and its BH is
     0.84. The
     licence is pre-specification; the text should say so once (it currently
     does not).
     7. "the overall proliferation signalling remained relatively stable" (2E) —
     right, with one
     qualifier. On the enrichment ruler it is as clean as it gets: not one of 
     the 14
     proliferation sets is significant on 6>12W_wt, median NES −0.89 against the
     TEB
     programmes' −1.56 with 30 of 41 significant. But on the gene-level ruler
     PROLIF_* pooled is
     −0.047 log2 at the 1.3rd percentile of its own matched null (p = 0.013) —
     small, not
     immobile, which is script 43's own phrase. Suggested: keep "relatively
     stable" and add the
     contrast that makes it quantitative (an 8.9-fold smaller move than the TEB
     arm).

     One trap to record: results/mitopps_scores.rds (12:35) is one minute older
     than
     gsva_scores.rds (12:36) although it was regenerated in the same
     post-reconciliation re-run, so
     require_fresher_than() would wrongly stop Fig. 1F. Gate 1F on
     interaction_results.rds
     instead and say why.

     ---
     Verification

     Per panel, before handing over: render through a scratchpad-redirected dry
     run
     (options(myc.fig.nosave = TRUE) + pdf(NULL)) so the whole draw pipeline
     executes and the
     repo tree is untouched; check the stopifnot assertions fire against the
     analysis of record;
     pdftotext the real PDF once written to confirm the base-pdf() device kept
     inter-word
     spacing. Then source(here::here("figures","panels","rebuild_panels.R")) to
     confirm the panel
     joins the set and its legend block lands in legends.md in slot order.

     Round 2 adds four checks, each of which is a stopifnot in the script rather
     than a thing to
     remember:

     1. The decomposition is exact, so the arrows mean what the panel says:
     assert
     max(abs(c_tn + c_int - c_tp)) == 0 and the same on the priority ruler. It
     holds today.
     2. The grammar is shared, not re-typed: every alternative starts from
     two_timeline_base(); assert the four panels use the same axis limits where
     they draw the
     same quantity.
     3. The chain heatmap's rows are the analysis of record: the coupling half
     must reproduce
     script 43's interaction to the same 1.2 % tolerance the current 2I already
     asserts, and the
     per-animal rows must come from axis_scores / expr_mat with
     identical(sample, colnames).
     4. Render every alternative through qlmanage, not sips — small filled
     circles come out as
     crosses under sips, and three of the four panels are dense scatters.

     Then source(panels_to_pdf.R) and confirm it writes three documents, with
     the two existing
     ones unchanged (same page counts: 12 and 8).

     Author runs them in Positron; I run them only if asked, as in the last
     round.

     Working rules that stay in force

     - Option A — I write scripts/ + figures/ + paper/; the author runs them in
     Positron,
     except where the author says "run it".
     - The publication rule — theme_panel() blanks title/subtitle/caption; every
     script carries
     a panel_legend() block; no prose on a panel.
     - Use the declared scheme — group_cols/group_labels, mito_class_*,
     ms_diverging,
     contrast_levels, programme_group(), mouse Myc. Anything new is declared in
     _panel_common.R, never inlined.
     - No new numbered analysis script is implied by any of the 14 panels; all
     read
     results/*.rds already on disk.
     - Git — ./paper/clean_icon_files.sh repo before the first git command
     (Drive mount);
     git mv for renames; leave
     docs/library_reference/gray_chea_mito_tf_shortlist.csv
     untracked; commit per signed-off panel; no force-push.

⏺ Four alternatives built, run, and committed (37c3e61) — 25/25 panels rebuild,
  and Figure2_alternatives.pdf (4 pages) now sits beside the unchanged
  Figure2_and_S2_panels.pdf.

  They share one plane: x = what development does across the window, y = what
  the Myc+ gland does, dashed diagonal = development alone. The device is exact
  rather than illustrative — script 40's ruler satisfies c_tp = c_tn + c_int to
  floating point, so a vertical drop from the diagonal is the interaction.
  That's what turns "more than the overall MYC effect decline" into something
  the reader sees.

  2F (alt) — development raises the compartment (median +0.041, 72% above zero)
  while stripping OXPHOS; Myc lowers everything (93% below the diagonal); OXPHOS
  ends deepest because the terms add (−0.255, then −0.150 more). Arrows draw
  that second term. The assembly-factor control sits at the origin.

  2G (alt) — Bbc3 is the only BH3-only sensor flat in development and lost under
  Myc (asserted). Bcl2l1 sits at the origin, so the ratio's reversal is all 
  numerator. Bmf sits on the diagonal — up in both genotypes — and is the
  panel's own negative control.

  2H+I (alt), merged as you chose — the chain per animal over the coupling. The 
  data corrected my premise mid-build: it is not a block that falls together.
  Foxo3 rises across the wild-type window and fails to rise under Myc; Bbc3 does
  the opposite. That's the interaction, and it's your own sentence. It's now
  asserted so a re-run can't invert it while the legend keeps claiming the old
  reading.

  S2D (alt) — the p53 arm, which is what you asked for: a band at y ≈ 0 while
  Bbc3 leaves the band and Foxo3 leaves the diagonal. Eda2r is named in the
  legend because it does move on the wild-type axis (−0.99, padj 0.019, 101
  counts) and the panel mustn't read flatter than it is.

  One text item worth acting on regardless of which panels you keep: Foxo3 is
  rank 1 and Bbc3 rank 2 of the 26 biogenesis/cell-death mechanism genes by 
  interaction p (0.0074, 0.0081, with a gap to the third at 0.066). That is the
  framing your reworded text already uses, and it is far stronger than the
  8,774-gene percentile the current 2H draws. Also: Bmf rises in both timelines
  and is the natural contrast to Bbc3 in the text, and Bax falls significantly
  under Myc — so "specific to Bbc3" wants "among the BH3-only sensors".
